- **stella**: PreToolUse, SessionEnd, SessionStart, UserPromptExpansion, UserPromptSubmit on Edit|MultiEdit|Write|Bash|PowerShell. BMad-Stella project hooks: friction-logger (session telemetry), pr-review-guard (blocks edits during PR review), memory (daily job + MEMORY.md inject). Scripts run against the session's project; all state is written under the project's _bmad-output/, never under the plugin root. ([Asrarul-BS23/BMad-Stella](https://github.com/Asrarul-BS23/BMad-Stella/blob/HEAD/stella/hooks/hooks.json))
